135|A|13. Productive - Habit Track|3271085937|us|3|2018-10| Kinda Good App. | In the past few days I’ve been looking for an app that could serve as a calendar and time tracker. However it doesn’t track my time on my activities. So I got this app to track my time.   What I find this app to be missing, is a calendar where I can see my schedule as I make it. For a person with ADHD, it is hard to visualize a schedule in the mind, it’s kinda like doing the “bara book bara boom” then never go on that vacation because I forgot about the whole thing. In the same manner I can schedule my intentions and even if there is a sound to remind me, if I can’t look at it on a visual timeline, the sound just feels like a reminder for something I’ve already forgotten and my self confidence gets shattered. There is absolutely zero purpose for my calendar to hang out there in a form of a text “to read later”. But if I want to clean my kitchen floor, I only have the option to schedule it with the same time and same day in in mind for the future. There is no “one time” scheduling on Productive. I usually make plans for such things only a day or two in advance and these chores don’t follow any time pattern.   What Productive is good for, are things that you want to be sure you are doing at the same time and same day every time you do them. It can be every day, same days a week, or for example, activities done every Wednesday each month. So what I use it for, is going to sleep at the same time every night (I need discipline to go to sleep), waking up at the same time every day at the same time and make sure my dog and I take our medications on time every day. I have two other activities on Productive that I want to make sure I make happen every day for 6 hours, 5 times a week. I would recommend that you don’t over schedule your Productive with activities, because you will still need time slots for your calendar. If you load all your activities on Productive, you will live like a robot. There are things that I need to do once in a while and they can’t interfere with activities on Productive. Since I live in a remote rural area I have to spend a day each month shopping for- and stocking on food to last for awhile. This shopping trip does not always fall on the same day or date and interferes with my Productive schedule. I guess that means I have to cheat once a month because there is no flexibility in the schedule on this app.  What is really good about Productive is that it’s simple to use (although the usefulness is quite limited in my opinion) and it is Siri activated.   The improvements I would most like to see is: 1) the ability to sync this app with my Apple calendar, 2) a bit more control over the timing of the schedules I create in Productive.
